Let's design better peak shaving.

For p length n normalized. Exact objective max c. We can reduce top peaks by local operations: choose a peak lag k with c_k near max. We can transfer a small amount δ from one coordinate j to l, update c_k exactly as δ( g_{l,k} - g_{j,k} ) plus second-order? For bilinear, c_k(p + ε(e_l - e_j)) = c_k + ε(a? p) ... If move mass from j to l, exact change linear in ε for fixed p? Actually p changes both factors; δ added to l, subtract from j. Change in c_k:
If k not affected? δ*( p_{k-l}^+ - p_{k-j}^+ + p_{k-l}^? include self terms 2δ p_{k-l} plus -δ p_{k-j} - p? plus δ^2 terms when both l and j involved. We can perform exact quadratic line search for a transfer δ along direction d = e_l - e_j with sum zero. Since objective max of quadratics, evaluate score at several δ via fast conv; choose best. This coordinate descent can be very effective, but full n coordinates too many. We can limit to top contributors to peaks and low contributors.

Previous coord_descent only reduced top contributors, not increase low. Adding to non-contributors may help. But it normalized after each move, which changes direction. Could do pairwise swap line search: transfer δ from a high p index that contributes to peak to a low p index not contributing. Score can be lowered if derivative for peak negative and other peaks not too high. Use multi-peak derivative: choose l with high marginal benefit (reduces many top peaks), j with high cost. We can compute gradient of LSE or max and do line search in direction d = v^+ - v^- (top positive gradient subtract, top negative gradient add). This is like Frank-Wolfe: direction from best negative gradient coordinate to best positive gradient coordinate? For minimizing f, subgradient g; decrease f by stepping in -g. On simplex, Frank-Wolfe direction picks argmin g (most negative) and argmax g (most positive) and step between. For nonconvex but can work.

Let's define objective F(p)=LSE_score(p,β). Gradient g normalized. On simplex, Frank-Wolfe: linear min over simplex: v = argmin g_i. Direction d = p - v. Lyapunov step. But p in simplex. L-BFGS also. We can implement "POWHEL" (projected Wolfe line search) but simpler: At each step, compute gradient of LSE. Select coordinate l with smallest gradient (increase), j with largest gradient (decrease), direction d_l +=1, d_j -=1 (sum zero). Then evaluate F(p + α d) with line search (alpha clipped to keep nonnegative), update. This is cheap (one gradient + few objective evals) and can move mass from high cost to low cost. It may be more direct than L-BFGS. But coordinate-wise may be slow; use top many: top P positive coordinates and top N negative, combine d = (top negative mean) - (top positive mean) and line search. This is like subgradient with momentum.

    n=len(a); a=normalize(a.copy()); best=compute_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       # optional exact score
       sc=2*n*cmax
       if sc<best: update
       shifted=beta*(c-cmax); clip; w=exp(shifted); w/=w.sum()
       grad = 2*n * 2? Let's compute gradient of L = LSE. lse_score_and_grad gives grad of score (including S). Since sum fixed 1, we can call lse_score_and_grad and take its grad? `fast_conv(a)`:
```
n=len(a)
m=2*n-1
M=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a, M)
b=np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:m]
# remove tiny negatives? max can be negative due to numerical; but nonneg a => conv nonneg. Clip maybe if used for max? Use np.maximum(b,0)
return b
```

Thus implement:
```
def grad_lse(p, w): # w length 2n-1
  r = p[::-1]
  m3 = len(w)+len(r)-1
  M = 1 << (m3-1).bit_length()
  W = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
  R = np.fft.rfft(r, M)
  h = np.fft.irfft(W*R, M)[:m3]
  return 2.0 * h[n-1:2*n-1]
```

```
def make_exchange_direction(g, p, rng, mode, topN):
    # g sum zero. We want pos = highest g (where increasing coordinate increases f), neg = lowest g.
    # Select topN positive and topN negative.
    pos_all = np.where(g>0)[0]
    neg_all = np.where(g<0)[0]
    if len(pos_all)==0 or len(neg_all)==0: return None
    if mode == 'top':
       pos = pos_all[np.argpartition(g[pos_all], -topN)[-topN:]]
       neg = neg_all[np.argpartition(g[neg_all], topN)[:topN]]
    else random sample from top larger set.
    # Optional weight by magnitude? d entries proportional to |g| to emphasize.
    masses: for neg, weights = -g[neg], normalized to 1; for pos, weights = g[pos], normalized to 1.
    d = zeros; d[neg] += weights_neg; d[pos] -= weights_pos.
    # Now sum nonzero approx 0? If weights neg sum=1, pos sum=1. Good.
    # Scale to total moved mass = step_mass? If weights sum=1, neg increase total 1, pos decrease total 1. But total mass cannot move 1 (100%); too large. Scale by m.
    return d * m
```

Implementation details top-level:

```
def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.zeros(0)
    m = 2*n -1
    M = 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:m]
    # numerical negative
    if b.size:
       b[ b < 0 ] = 0.0? Setting all negatives to 0 may hide small but okay for max; but if actual conv nonnegative, negative only numerical. For gradient weights, negative tiny irrelevant. Use np.maximum(b,0,out=b).
    return b
```
`compute_score(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
n=len(a)
S=a.sum()
if S<1e-12: return inf
c=fast_conv(a)
M=c.max()
return 2.0*n*float(M)/(S*S)
```

```
def lse_score_and_grad(a,beta):
    n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a); S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return 1e100, zeros
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    sh=beta*(c-cmax); sh=np.clip(sh,-700,700)
    e=np.exp(sh); e=np.clip(e,1e-300,None)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300: e[cmax_index]=1; s=1
    w=e/s
    L=cmax+np.log(s)/beta
    gradL=grad_lse(a,w)
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    grad=2*n*(gradL/S2 - 2*L/S3)
    val=2*n*L/S2
    return val, grad
```
If beta=0? We only call beta>0. If beta small, L approx mean c; fine.

`grad_lse(a,w)` as above.

`run_lbfgs(a0,beta,maxiter,maxfun,start_score?)`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0,beta,maxiter=2000,maxfun=5000):
    a=normalize(a0.copy())
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*len(a)
    x0=a.copy()
    try:
      res=minimize(lse_score_and_grad,x0,args=(beta,),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxcor':20,'maxls':20})
      aout=np.clip(res.x,0,1000)
      if aout.sum()<1e-12: aout=a
      sc=compute_score(aout)
      return sc,aout
    except: return compute_score(a),a
```

def log_param_from_p(p):

Let's think about `exchange_descent` performance and tuning in detail.

```
def exchange_descent(a, beta, max_iters, topN, mass_choices, deadline, rng, label):
    n=len(a); a=normalize(a.copy())
    best=compute_score(a); best_a=a.copy(); stagnant=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a)
       cmax=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*cmax
       if sc<best-1e-12: best=sc; best_a=a.copy(); stagnant=0
       else: stagnant+=1
       # gradient fixed beta
       shift=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(np.clip(shift,-700,700)); e[ np.log? ] ensure sum
       if e.sum()==0: break
       w=e/e.sum()
       g=2*n*grad_lse(a,w)
       g = g - g.mean()
       # select sets with rng, mode varies with it/stagnant
       d, alpha_max = build_direction(g,a,rng,topN,it)
       if d is None: break
       # candidate alpha
       for mass in mass_choices:
           amax = min(alpha_max, mass)
           for frac in [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
              alpha=frac*amax
              p_new=a+alpha*d
              if p_new min < -1e-12: continue
              p_new=np.maximum(p_new,0); s=p_new.sum(); if s<1e-15 continue; p_new /= s
              sc2=compute_score(p_new)
              if sc2<sc - 1e-12: # accept best
                 ...
       if no improvement: maybe do random perturbation of constant scale? If stagnant > 20: a=perturb_mult(a,rng,scale=0.002+...), normalize, best maybe not update (could rise). But exchange from perturb maybe; However best_a preserved. We can set working a to perturb to escape, but if it raises score and later improves below current? Need return best. Good.
    return best_a, best
```
Handling working `a` vs best: If we perturb `a` to escape, it may be worse than current best; new local search may find a new basin. We should track best separately. But if a becomes too bad (score much higher), exchange may take long. Use small perturb and cap: only if score(a) < best*1.05? Or use random subset direction rather than full perturb.

We can periodically (every 100 stagnant) reset current a to best_a but change rng/topN/mass. No quality loss. This is like deterministic restart within local. Good.

Build direction details:
```
def build_exchange_direction(g,a,rng,topN,variant):
    # replace zeros? p positive.
    pos_mask = g > 1e-12
    neg_mask = g < -1e-12
    n=len(a)
    if not any pos/neg: return None, 0
    # candidate pool: top 10*topN by magnitude among pos/neg; choose topN with some random
    pos_idx = np.where(g>0)[0]
    neg_idx = np.where(g<0)[0]
    if len(pos_idx)==0 or len(neg_idx)==0: return None,0
    kpos=min(topN,len(pos_idx)); kneg=min(topN,len(neg_idx))
    # Use scores g positive, -g negative. To include randomness, sample from top_frac.
    if variant%3==0:
        pos = pos_idx[np.argpartition(g[pos_idx], -int(kpos*5))[-kpos:]] if len>...
    etc.
    # weights proportional g magnitude but cap by mass? We'll use wpos = g[pos]/sum(g[pos]); wneg = -g[neg]/sum(-g[neg])

    return d, alpha_nonneg
```

Pseudo:
```
def exchange_mass(a, beta, max_iters, deadline, rng):
    a=normalize(a); best=score; best_a=a
    it=0; stagnant=0
    while it<max_iters and time<deadline:
       c=fast_conv(a); cmax=c.max(); sc=2*n*cmax
       if sc<best: update best/stagnant=0 else stagnant++
       compute g from LSE with beta (maybe high) tangent not needed for selection? g=2n gradL (no subtract mean). Use g values.
       best_iter_score=sc
       # generate candidates e.g. 8
       for candidate:
          pnew = apply_candidate(a,g,rng,params)
          if valid: sc2=compute_score(pnew)
          if sc2<best_iter_score: accept pnew, best_iter_score=sc2; a=pnew
       it++
       if stagnant>100: # restart current from best but with perturbation
           a=best_a.copy()
           if score(a) ...
           stochastic perturbation small
    return best_a,best
```
We should accept only improvement over current `a` (like local hill-climbing). If no improvement, leave a; stagnant. To explore, sometimes accept equal or worse with annealing? Could maintain current a as best to avoid climbing. But local minima may need small worsens; use random perturbations periodically from best. Pure hill-climbing can get stuck in plateau. Let's implement `fienup_flat` and `fienup_quantile` quickly:
Previous versions compute score every iter; can be heavy. Use fewer and maybe only for starts. Implement top-level:
```
def fienup_flat(a, n_iters=100):
  n=len(a); a=normalize(a); M=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length(); best=score; best_a=a
  for it in range(n_iters):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    P=A.real**2+A.imag**2
    Pmean=P.mean()
    alpha=0.2+0.6*(it/n_iters)
    P_new=(1-alpha)*P+alpha*Pmean
    P_new=np.maximum(P_new,1e-30)
    ratio=np.sqrt(P_new/P+1e-30)
    A_new=A*ratio
    a_new=np.fft.irfft(A_new,M)[:n]
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0); a_new=normalize(a_new)
    sc=compute_score(a_new)
    if sc<best: best=sc; best_a=a_new.copy()
  return best_a
```
Quantile similar. This may improve. Use 50-100 iter from parent.

- `perturb_add(a,rng,scale)`: a + scale*normal, normalize.
- `perturb_mult_freq(a,rng,scale)`: a*(1+sum cos/sin low freq), normalize.
- `mix_sequences(a,b,alpha)`: normalize(alpha*a+(1-alpha)b). Could mix parent with its reverse? Reverse same score; with perturb.

Let's define:
```
def exchange_mass(a, beta, max_iters, deadline, rng):
  cur=normalize(a.copy()); cur_score=compute_score(cur); best=cur_score; best_a=cur; noimp=0
  it=0
  while it<max_iters and time<deadline:
    c=fast_conv(cur); cmax=c.max(); sc=2*n*cmax
    if sc < cur_score - 1e-12: cur_score=sc
    if sc < best - 1e-12: best=sc; best_a=cur.copy()
    # gradient
    shift=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(np.clip(shift,-700,700)); es=e.sum(); if es<... break; w=e/es; g=2*n*grad_lse(cur,w)
    # orders
    pos_score = np.maximum(g*cur, 0.0)
    pos_order_ga = np.argsort(-pos_score)
    pos_order_g = np.argsort(-np.maximum(g,0.0))
    neg_order = np.argsort(np.minimum(g,0.0)) # descending g (negative most first) or -g
    # generate candidate parameter chosen by it and rng
    # evaluate up to 3 candidates
    itimp=False
    for param in choose_params(it,rng):
       pnew=make_candidate(cur, pos_order_ga, pos_order_g, neg_order, param)
       if pnew is None: continue
       sc2=compute_score(pnew)
       if sc2 < cur_score - 1e-12:
           cur=pnew; cur_score=sc2; itimp=True
           if sc2 < best - 1e-12: best=sc2; best_a=pnew.copy()
       # optional equal accept?
    if not itimp:
       noimp +=1
       if noimp > 40:
          noimp=0
          cur=best_a + 0.0005*?; cur=normalize(perturb(cur,...)); cur_score=compute_score(cur)
          # don't update best if worsened
    else: noimp=0
    it+=1
  return best_a,best,cur,cur_score
```

Let's detail each.

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0,dtype=np.float64)
    m=2*n-1
    M=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:m]
    # remove tiny negative
    if b.size and b.min()<0:
       b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return b
```
Repeated allocation b=max. Could use `np.maximum(b,0,out=b)` but if b min>=0 no. Use in-place.
`b = np.ifft?` okay.

`compute_score`:
```
def compute_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M<0: M=0
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```

`normalize`:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-15:
       return np.ones_like(a)/max(1,a.size)
    a=a/S
    return a
```
If zeros all, uniform.

`grad_lse`:
```
def grad_lse(a,w):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    r=a[::-1]
    m3=len(w)+n-1
    M=1 << (m3-1).bit_length()
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,M)
    R=np.fft.rfft(r,M)
    h=np.fft.irfft(W*R,M)[:m3]
    return 2.0*h[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Could have h tiny imaginary? irfft real. okay.

`lse_score_q_and_grad(q,beta,order)`:
```
def lse_score_q_and_grad(q,beta,order):
    q=np.asarray(q,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    order=np.asarray(order,dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    n=order.size
    if n==0: return 1e100, np.zeros(0)
    # build p from q/order
    pbuild=build_p_from_q(q, order)
    val,gp=lse_score_and_grad(pbuild,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100, np.zeros(len(q))
    dot=float(np.dot(pbuild,gp))
    gz_full=pbuild*(gp-dot)
    grad=q? grad_q = gz_full[order[:-1]]
    return float(val), grad_q
```
`build_p_from_q(q,order)`:
```
def build_p_from_q(q,order):
    n=order.size
    p=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n==1:
       p[order[0]]=1.0
       return p
    qmax=np.max(q) if q.size else 0.0
    zmax=max(0.0, qmax)
    qs = q - zmax
    qs = np.clip(qs, -700.0, 700.0)
    e=np.exp(qs)
    base=np.exp(-zmax) if zmax < 700.0 else 0.0
    denom=float(e.sum()) + base
    if denom <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(denom):
        p[order[-1]]=1.0
        return p
    p[order[:-1]] = e/denom
    p[order[-1]] = base/denom
    # clip tiny
    p = np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s>0: p/=s
    return p
```
Need if zmax=0, base=1. Good. If q large positive, base 0.

`log_param_from_p(p)`:
```
def log_param_from_p(p):
    p=normalize(p)
    n=p.size
    b=int(np.argmax(p))
    order=np.arange(n,dtype=np.int64)
    order = np.concatenate((order[order != b], np.array([b], dtype=np.int64)))
    base = max(float(p[b]), 1e-300)
    vals = p[order[:-1]]
    q = np.log(np.maximum(vals, 1e-300)) - np.log(base)
    q = np.clip(q, -700.0, 700.0)
    return q, order
```
If n=1, q empty, order [0]. L-BFGS on empty? Need handle: If n=1, return score. But n large.

`run_lbfgs_q(a0,beta,maxiter,maxfun)`:
```
def run_lbfgs_q(a0,beta,maxiter=2000,maxfun=5000):
    a=normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<=1: return compute_score(a),a
    sc0=compute_score(a)
    q0,order=log_param_from_p(a)
    if q0.size==0: return sc0,a
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_score_q_and_grad,q0,args=(beta,order),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,options={'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-14,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxcor':10,'maxls':10})
       qopt=res.x
       aout=build_p_from_q(qopt,order)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(aout)) or aout.sum()<1e-15:
          return sc0,a
       scout=compute_score(aout)
       return scout,aout
    except Exception:
       return sc0,a
```

Could call q with a moderate beta; if improves, continue. Use p for final polish if q struggles? p objective with bounds can handle nonzeros. We can implement but time maybe. Let's implement `run_lbfgs_p` and use if q fails/worse. But objective logic same. In p L-BFGS, variable length n bounds [0,1000]; starting p normalized. It can change sum and zeros. `fienup_flat/quantile`: Need ensure no large time. Use `M_fft = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()` (length of conv), same as fast_conv. For each iter, compute A, P. Use `ratio = np.sqrt(P_new / np.maximum(P,1e-30))`. To keep a real nonnegative, after irfft clip. Evaluate score. Return best.

```
def fienup_flat(a,n_iters=80,deadline=None):
   a=normalize(a); n=a.size; M=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length(); best=compute_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
   for it in range(n_iters):
     if deadline and time>deadline: break
     A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
     P=np.abs(A)**2
     Pmean=float(P.mean())
     alpha=0.15+0.65*(it/max(1,n_iters))
     Pnew=(1-alpha)*P + alpha*Pmean
     Pnew=np.maximum(Pnew,1e-30)
     ratio=np.sqrt(Pnew/np.maximum(P,1e-30))
     Anew=A*ratio
     anew=np.fft.irfft(Anew,M)[:n]
     anew=normalize(anew)
     sc=compute_score(anew)
     if sc<best: best=sc; best_a=anew.copy()
   return best_a
```
Quantile use thresh.
Maybe `fienup_flat` can increase score; we return best.

`perturb_mult`:
```
def perturb_mult(a,rng,scale):
    n=a.size
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)/max(1,n)
    pert=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1,5):
       f=int(rng.integers(1,15))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0,1))*2*np.pi
       amp=scale/(m+0.5)
       pert += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
       pert += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x+ph+0.7)
    new=a*(1+pert)
    return normalize(new)
```
`perturb_add` similar.

`interpolate_sequence` as previous.

`make_exchange_candidate`: Need top-level. Detailed implementation:
```
def make_exchange_candidate(a,g,pos_order_ga,pos_order_g,neg_order,kpos,kadd,frac,pos_mode,add_mode):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return None
    kpos=min(int(kpos), max(1,n//2))
    kadd=min(int(kadd), max(1,n))
    if pos_mode==0:
        pos_all=pos_order_ga
    else:
        pos_all=pos_order_g
    # pos_all may contain indices with pos_score 0 at end; take first kpos, filter a>1e-12
    pos = pos_all[:kpos]
    pos = pos[a[pos] > 1e-12]
    if pos.size==0: return None
    # If selected mass very small, try expand up to min(4*kpos, n//2) by taking more from pos_all
    if a[pos].sum() < 1e-5 and pos.size < pos_all.size:
        extra = min(pos_all.size, max(2*kpos, kpos+min(kpos,16)))
        pos = pos_all[:extra]
        pos = pos[a[pos] > 1e-12]
        if pos.size==0: return None
    frac = float(min(0.5, max(1e-4, frac)))
    pnew = a.copy()
    rem = frac * a[pos]
    m = float(rem.sum())
    if m < 1e-12: return None
    pnew[pos] = a[pos] - rem
    neg = neg_order[:kadd]
    neg = neg[g[neg] < -1e-12]
    if neg.size==0: return None
    if add_mode==0:
       weights=np.ones(neg.size)
    elif add_mode==1:
       weights=-g[neg]
    else:
       weights=(-g[neg])*(a[neg]+1e-8)
    weights=np.asarray(weights,dtype=np.float64)
    weights[weights<0]=0
    wsum=float(weights.sum())
    if wsum <= 1e-30:
       weights=np.ones(neg.size); wsum=float(neg.size)
    add = m * weights / wsum
    mean_a=1.0/n
    max_add = min(0.005, max(0.0002, 50.0*mean_a))
    if add.size and add.max() > max_add:
       add = np.minimum(add, max_add)
       # redistribute up to 2 times
       for _ in range(3):
          deficit = m - float(add.sum())
          if deficit <= 1e-14: break
          mask = add < max_add
          if not mask.any(): break
          wmask = weights[mask]
          wsumm = float(wmask.sum())
          if wsumm <= 1e-30: 
             extra_vector = 1.0/mask.sum()
             add[mask] += deficit * extra_vector
          else:
             add[mask] += deficit * wmask / wsumm
       # If still over/under, accept
    pnew[neg] = pnew[new? neg] + add  # careful: pnew[neg] += add works with advanced indexing? Yes in numpy, pnew[neg] += add buffered may duplicate if neg duplicates but no duplicates. It works but with advanced indexing it may not be in-place? Actually `pnew[neg] += add` does get-then-set, fine for unique indices. Use `pnew[neg] = pnew[neg] + add`.
    pnew = np.maximum(pnew,0.0)
    s=float(pnew.sum())
    if s < 1e-12: return None
    pnew = pnew/s
    return pnew
```
Need `pos_all` arrays sorted, can contain positive g only? We'll build in exchange so yes. But after filtering, if pos includes indices with g<=0? We sort by score maybe zero; filtering a>0. Could include g=0. Fine.

`exchange_mass` uses this. Need be careful `pos_order_ga` can be all n indices. If `kpos` > size. fine.

```
def exchange_mass(a, beta, max_iters, deadline, rng, num_cands=2):
    n=a.size; a=normalize(a.copy()); cur_score=compute_score(a); best=cur_score; best_a=a.copy(); noimp=0
    it=0
    while it<max_iters and time<deadline:
       c=fast_conv(a); cmax=float(c.max()); sc=2.0*n*cmax
       if sc < cur_score - 1e-12: cur_score=sc
       if sc < best - 1e-12: best=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       # weights
       shift=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(np.clip(shift,-700,700)); es=e.sum()
       if es<=0: break
       w=e/es
       g=2.0*n*grad_lse(a,w)
       # orders
       g_pos=np.maximum(g,0.0)
       pos_order_g=np.argsort(-g_pos)
       pos_score=g_pos*a
       pos_order_ga=np.argsort(-pos_score)
       neg_order=np.argsort(-np.minimum(g,0.0))
       improved=False
       # choose cands
       choose_idx = int(rng.integers(len(EXCHANGE_PARAMS)))
       for _ in range(num_cands):
           param=EXCHANGE_PARAMS[choose_idx % len(EXCHANGE_PARAMS)]
           choose_idx = int(rng.integers(len(EXCHANGE_PARAMS)))
           pnew=make_exchange_candidate(a,g,pos_order_ga,pos_order_g,neg_order,*param)
           if pnew is None: continue
           sc2=compute_score(pnew)
           if sc2 < cur_score - 1e-12:
               a=pnew; cur_score=sc2; improved=True
               if sc2 < best - 1e-12: best=sc2; best_a=pnew.copy()
           # If not improved but sc2<best? impossible if cur maybe >best? cur_score may be worse than best due to previous perturb? If cur_score worse, a candidate could improve over cur but not best. The condition sc2<cur accepts. Good.
       if improved: noimp=0
       else:
          noimp +=1
          if noimp >= 30:
             noimp=0
             # Try a small perturbation of best_a
             if best_score? variable name
             a=perturb_mult(best_a,rng,scale=0.002 + 0.001*rng.random())
             cur_score=compute_score(a)
             # If perturbed is much worse (>best*1.02), reset to best without perturbation
             if cur_score > best * 1.01 and cur_score > 2.0:
                 a=best_a.copy(); cur_score=best
             elif cur_score > best * 1.05:
                 a=perturb_add(best_a,rng,scale=0.0005); cur_score=compute_score(a)
    return best_a,best,a,cur_score
```

1. Create start candidates:

`make_initial_no_parent(n,rng)`:
Create a mixture of profiles, optimize? We can just generate a few profiles and return best among them (quick score) maybe uniform. But final local can improve.
Profiles:
- uniform: ones/n
- power increasing: x^a normalized for a in [0.5,1,2]
- truncated exponential with λ [0.5,1,2]
- asymmetric two gaussian: weights. Evaluate quick and pick best. Since no parent maybe low score. But likely parent.
Implement:
```
def make_initial_no_parent(n,rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    candidates=[]
    candidates.append(np.ones(n))
    for power in [0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5,2.0]:
       candidates.append((x**power))
       candidates.append(((1-x)**power))
    for lam in [0.5,1.0,2.0]:
       candidates.append(np.exp(-lam*x))
       candidates.append(np.exp(-lam*(1-x)))
    # gaussian two hump
    for c,w in [(0.25,0.1),(0.75,0.1)]: candidates.append(np.exp(-((x-c)/w)**2)+0.2*np.ones(n))
    best_a=None; best=inf
    for cand in candidates:
       p=normalize(cand)
       sc=compute_score(p)
       if sc<best: best=sc; best_a=p
    # mix best with random perturb
    return best_a
```
This may produce score <1? For n small? no matter.

Let's include a simple `pair_swap_optimize` as local polish. It can be cheap and provide different moves than value transfers. It can escape value local minima. Implementation:
```
def pair_swap_optimize(a, max_iters, deadline, rng):
  n=len(a); a=normalize(a); best=score; best_a=a; it=0; noimp=0
  while it<max_iters and time<deadline:
     # candidate pairs
     improved=False
     for _ in range(4):
        i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
        if i==j: continue
        # optional bias: choose from top gradient? no
        pnew=a.copy(); pnew[i],pnew[j]=a[j],a[i]
        sc2=compute_score(pnew)
        if sc2 < compute_score(a) - 1e-12: accept
     if no improve for 30: perturb or reset
     it++
  return best
```

`lse_score_and_grad`: if `beta` is not finite? Could set. We'll call float.
```
def lse_score_and_grad(a,beta):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return 1e100, np.zeros(0)
   c=fast_conv(a); S=float(a.sum())
   if S<1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
   beta=max(1e-8,float(beta))
   shifted=beta*(c-cmax)
   shifted=np.clip(shifted,-700,700)
   e=np.exp(shifted)
   es=float(e.sum())
   if es<1e-300 or not finite:
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); e=np.zeros_like(c); e[idx]=1; es=1
   w=e/es
   L=cmax + np.log(es)/beta
   gradL=grad_lse(a,w)
   S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
   grad=2.0*n*(gradL/S2 - 2.0*L/S3)
   val=float(2.0*n*L/S2)
   if not np.isfinite(val) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
      return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
   return val, grad
```
For p from q sum 1, S=1; gradient okay. For L-BFGS p with bounds, starting normalized, x may become zero? S terms. If x near zero, val 1e100 gradient zero; L-BFGS may bad; but has bounds and start ok.

`lse_score_q_and_grad`: If build p has p sum1; val finite. Use gp from generic; but generic gradient w.r.t p includes scale; at p sum1 fine. Dot maybe.
```
def lse_score_q_and_grad(q,beta,order):
    q=np.asarray(q,float).ravel()
    order=np.asarray(order,int).ravel()
    n=order.size
    if n<=1: return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0,n-1))
    p=build_p_from_q(q,order)
    val,gp=lse_score_and_grad(p,beta)
    if not finite or gp.size != n: return 1e100, zeros
    dot=float(np.dot(p,gp))
    grad_full=p*(gp-dot)
    grad_q=grad_full[order[:-1]]
    if not finite: grad_q=zeros
    return val, grad_q
```
`gp` from generic if S not exactly 1? yes.
Potential: If p is constructed sum1 but due to q baseline, dot maybe slight, okay.

def run_lbfgs_p(a0,beta,maxiter,maxfun):
   a=normalize(a0); sc0=compute_score(a)
   bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*a.size
   try: res=minimize(lse_score_and_grad,a0,args=(beta,),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,options=...)
   ...
```

Now, `optimize_start`: Need possible `budget_s` small. We can implement robust.
```
def optimize_start(a, budget_s, deadline, rng, verbose=False):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    stop=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    if stop>deadline: stop=deadline
    a=normalize(a.copy())
    best=compute_score(a); best_a=a.copy(); cur=a.copy()
    if verbose: print
    # L-BFGS betas
    lbfgs_betas = [8.0, 30.0, 120.0, 500.0, 2000.0]
    for idx,beta in enumerate(lbfgs_betas):
       if time.monotonic() >= stop or budget_s ==0: break
       rem=stop-time
       if rem < 5: break
       # allocate roughly equal but max 30
       seg = min(35.0, rem/ max(1, len(lbfgs_betas)-idx))
       maxiter=int(min(2500, max(300, seg*5)))
       sc,p=run_lbfgs_q(cur,beta,maxiter, int(max(3000, maxiter*3)))
       if sc<best: best=sc; best_a=p.copy(); cur=p.copy()
       # maybe if no improvement, try p optimizer once on best for medium beta
       if idx in (1,3) and stop-time > seg*1.5:
           sc2,p2=run_lbfgs_p(best_a,beta*2,int(max(300,min(1500,seg*3))), int(max(2000,min(5000,seg*8))))
           if sc2<best: best=sc2; best_a=p2; cur=p2
    # Exchange betas
    ex_betas=[30.0,150.0,700.0,2500.0,8000.0]
    for idx,beta in enumerate(ex_betas):
       if time >= stop: break
       rem=stop-time
       if rem<5: break
       seg=min(120.0, rem/max(1,len(ex_betas)-idx))
       maxit=int(min(50000,max(500,seg*20))) # if seg 100 ->2000
       b_a,b_sc,cur,cur_sc=exchange_mass(cur,beta,maxit,stop,rng, num_cands=2 if beta<2500 else 1)
       if b_sc<best: best=b_sc; best_a=b_a.copy()
       cur=best_a.copy()
    # final L-BFGS
    rem=stop-time
    if rem>10:
       maxiter=int(min(3000,max(400,rem*4)))
       sc,p=run_lbfgs_q(best_a,5000,maxiter,int(max(3000,maxiter*2)))
       if sc<best: best=sc; best_a=p
       rem=stop-time
       if rem>8:

    return best_a,best

Let's write `exchange_mass` fully:
```
def exchange_mass(a, beta, max_iters, deadline, rng, num_cands=2):
    a=normalize(a.copy())
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a, np.inf, a, np.inf
    cur_score=compute_score(a)
    best_score=cur_score
    best_a=a.copy()
    it=0
    no_imp=0
    param_cycle=EXCHANGE_PARAMS
    while it < max_iters and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c=fast_conv(a)
        cmax=float(np.max(c))
        sc=2.0*n*cmax
        if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
            cur_score=sc
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
        shift=beta*(c-cmax)
        shift=np.clip(shift,-700,700)
        e=np.exp(shift)
        es=float(e.sum())
        if es <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(es):
            break
        w=e/es
        g=2.0*n*grad_lse(a,w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        grange=float(np.max(g)-np.min(g))
        if grange <= 1e-14 * max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(g)))):
            # force a random perturbation to escape flat gradient
            no_imp += 5
        gpos=np.maximum(g,0.0)
        pos_order_g=np.argsort(-gpos)
        pos_score=gpos*a
        pos_order_ga=np.argsort(-pos_score)
        neg_order=np.argsort(-np.minimum(g,0.0))
        improved=False
        cidx = int(rng.integers(len(param_cycle)))
        for cand_idx in range(num_cands):
             param=param_cycle[cidx % len(param_cycle)]
             cidx = int(rng.integers(len(param_cycle)))
             pnew=make_exchange_candidate(a,g,pos_order_ga,pos_order_g,neg_order, param[0], param[1], param[2], param[3], param[4])
             if pnew is None: continue
             sc2=compute_score(pnew)
             if sc2 < cur_score - 1e-12:
                 a=pnew
                 cur_score=sc2
                 improved=True
                 if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                     best_score=sc2; best_a=pnew.copy()
             elif sc2 < best_score - 1e-12 and sc2 < cur_score? If cur worse than best, accept if improves cur anyway. The condition sc2<cur handles. If cur much worse, any improvement accepted. Good.
        if improved:
           no_imp=0
        else:
           no_imp +=1
           if no_imp >= 25:
               no_imp=0
               # escalate perturbation
               scale = 0.001 + 0.003 * min(1.0, (it % 1000)/1000.0)
               a2=perturb_mult(best_a,rng,scale)
               sc2=compute_score(a2)
               # If perturbation is not too bad, use it; if too bad, try additive small
               if sc2 < best_score * 1.02 and sc2 < cur_score * 1.05:
                   a=a2; cur_score=sc2
               else:
                   a2=perturb_add(best_a,rng,0.0005)
                   sc2=compute_score(a2)
                   if sc2 < cur_score:
                       a=a2; cur_score=sc2
                   else:
                       a=best_a.copy(); cur_score=best_score
        it +=1
    return best_a,best_score,a,cur_score
```
Need if `it` exceeds? okay. But if gradient flat and no perturb, no_imp increments. Good.
`param_cycle` is global list; `rng.integers` returns np int; okay.

Potential issue: `perturb_mult` uses `rng.integers` and returns p sum1. If `best_a` has zeros, multiplying by zero stays zero; but add can? perturb_mult doesn't add zeros. Fine.

In `make_exchange_candidate`, if `pos_order_ga` sorted by g*a, if all g positive but some negative? pos_order_g full includes all indices, positive first. `pos = pos_all[:kpos]`; if kpos > positive count, includes zeros; filter g>1e-12. Good.
`neg_order=np.argsort(-np.minimum(g,0.0))`; if all g positive, min=0 all; neg filter none. Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try: seed=int(seed)
    except: seed=42
    try: budget_s=float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s=2400.0
    if budget_s < 120: budget_s=120.0
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-30.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # parent
    a0=None
    try:
       h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if h is not None:
          arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
          if arr.size>=16 and np.sum(arr) > 1e-12 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
              a0=normalize(arr)
    except Exception: a0=None
    if a0 is None:
       a0=make_initial_no_parent(8192,rng)
    best_score=compute_score(a0); best_a=a0.copy()
    print(...)
    start_list=[(best_score,a0.copy())]
    # generate starts if time
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 300:
       # Fienup
       fa=fienup_flat(a0, n_iters=40)
       start_list.append((compute_score(fa), fa))
       fq=fienup_quantile(a0, n_iters=40)
       start_list.append((compute_score(fq), fq))
       p1=perturb_mult(a0,rng,0.03); start_list.append((compute_score(p1), p1))
       p2=perturb_mult(a0,rng,0.08); start_list.append((compute_score(p2), p2))
       p3=perturb_add(a0,rng,0.01); start_list.append((compute_score(p3), p3))
       p4=interpolate_sequence(a0, max(64, int(len(a0)*0.8)))? maybe not, reduces n. skip.
    # sort starts by score
    start_list.sort(key=start_sort_key)
    # Ensure parent maybe not first if another better; but keep parent included. We'll pick final_starts = [start_list[0]] + [s for s in start_list[1:4]]
    final_starts=start_list[:4]
    if len(final_starts) < 4: final_starts.extend? no.
    # remove duplicates? If same object? no.
    safe = max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    # reserve final 10%
    reserve = min(250.0, max(60.0, safe * 0.10))
    start_budget = max(60.0, safe - reserve)
    nuse=min(4,len(final_starts))
    weights=[0.42,0.26,0.18,0.14][:nuse]
    wsum=sum(weights)
    for idx in range(nuse):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10: break
       alloc = start_budget * weights[idx] / wsum
       sc_start,a_start=final_starts[idx]
       # If start score is much worse than current best and not parent? Maybe reduce allocation.
       if idx>0 and sc_start > best_score + 0.1:
           alloc *= 0.5
       # ensure not exceed remaining
       alloc=min(alloc, deadline-time.monotonic())
       if alloc < 15: continue
       b_a,b_sc=optimize_start(a_start, alloc, deadline, rng, verbose=(idx==0))
       if b_sc < best_score:
           best_score=b_sc; best_a=b_a.copy(); print
    # large n interpolation if time
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180:
       n_cur=len(best_a)
       if n_cur < 12000:
          target_n = min(12000, int(round(n_cur*1.3)))
          if target_n > n_cur + 512:
             a_large=interpolate_sequence(best_a,target_n)
             sc_large=compute_score(a_large)
             if sc_large < best_score:
                 best_score=sc_large; best_a=a_large
             # optimize large if time
             if time.monotonic() < deadline - 100:
                 b_a,b_sc=optimize_start(best_a, min(180.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-50), deadline, rng, verbose=False)
                 if b_sc<best_score: update
       elif n_cur < 18000 and rem > 240:
          a_large=interpolate_sequence(best_a, int(min(18000, n_cur*1.3)))
          ...
    # final reserve optimize
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40:
       b_a,b_sc=optimize_start(best_a, max(10.0, rem-15.0), deadline, rng, verbose=True)
       if b_sc<best_score: update
    # output
    n=len(best_a)
    scale=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    out_arr=np.clip(best_a,0.0,1000.0/scale? Actually best_a* scale may exceed 1000 if p_i huge. Clip after multiply.
    out_arr=best_a * scale
    mask=(out_arr<0); out_arr[mask]=0; out_arr[out_arr>1000]=1000
    out=[float(x) for x in out_arr]
    if sum(out)<0.01: out=[1.0]*64
    try: verified=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except: verified=best_score
    print
    return out

Now, one important mathematical thing: The objective score is scale invariant, but in L-BFGS q, softmax p sum1. The objective value val =2n LSE/S^2 =2n LSE. The gradient wrt q uses gp (gradient wrt p of val). We used `dot=p*gp`. Correct. However `lse_score_and_grad` generic includes S terms. For p from softmax, variations in q do not change sum exactly (to first order sum=1 but second order? The sum of softmax p is exactly 1 for all q, so derivative of sum wrt q = ∑ dp =0. The chain rule derivative of val (which depends on p only) is ∑∂val/∂p_i dp_i. Using gp generic gradient (including S terms) evaluated at p sum=1 is fine. Since p is on manifold, the gradient of restricted function over namesp is gp projected, but the softmax derivative formula already projects. Good.

```
def quick_polish(a, budget_s, deadline, rng):
   stop=min(deadline,time+budget)
   for beta in [100,1000,5000]:
      rem=stop-time; if rem<10 break
      maxit=int(max(1000, rem*30))
      b_a,b_sc,cur,cur_sc=exchange_mass(a,beta,maxit,stop,rng,1)
      if b_sc<best: best=b_sc; best_a=b_a; a=best_a
   return best_a,best
```

```
def quick_exchange_polish(a,budget_s,deadline,rng,verbose=False):
   stop=min(deadline, time+budget)
   a=normalize(a); best=compute_score(a); best_a=a; cur=a
   betas=(50.0,300.0,1500.0,6000.0)
   for beta in betas:
      if time>=stop: break
      rem=stop-time; if rem<10: break
      maxit=int(min(100000,max(300, rem*25)))
      b_a,b_sc,cur,cur_sc=exchange_mass(cur,beta,maxit,stop,rng,num_cands=1)
      if b_sc<best: best=b_sc; best_a=b_a.copy(); cur=best_a
   return best_a,best
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
score = 2n * max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The method combines:
- stochastic local mass-exchange with LSE-scaled autocorrelation gradients,
- L-BFGS in a softmax/tangent parameterization,
- Fienup-style spectral perturbations for diversity,
- limited larger-n interpolation/exploration.

The returned sequence is scaled so that the reported value is invariant
under global rescaling.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


EXCHANGE_PARAMS = (
    (8, 16, 0.05, 0, 1),
    (16, 32, 0.10, 1, 0),
    (16, 32, 0.20, 0, 1),
    (32, 64, 0.05, 0, 2),
    (32, 64, 0.15, 1, 1),
    (64, 128, 0.10, 0, 0),
    (64, 128, 0.25, 0, 1),
    (128, 256, 0.05, 1, 2),
    (128, 256, 0.20, 0, 1),
    (256, 512, 0.05, 0, 0),
    (256, 512, 0.15, 1, 1),
    (512, 1024, 0.05, 0, 2),
    (512, 1024, 0.10, 0, 0),
    (1024, 2048, 0.05, 1, 1),
    (2048, 4096, 0.05, 0, 0),
    (4096, 8192, 0.03, 0, 0),
)


def start_sort_key(item):
    return float(item[0])


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution by real FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:m]
    if b.size and b.min() < 0.0:
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def compute_score(a):
    """Exact score for a nonnegative sequence (scale invariant)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def normalize(a):
    """Clip to [0, 1000] and normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / S


def grad_lse(a, w):
    """
    Gradient of L = LSE_beta(c(a,a)) with respect to a.
    Here w are the softmax weights over the convolution coefficients.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    r = a[::-1]
    m3 = len(w) + n - 1
    M = 1 << (m3 - 1).bit_length()
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    R = np.fft.rfft(r, M)
    h = np.fft.irfft(W * R, M)[:m3]
    return 2.0 * h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def lse_score_and_grad(a, beta):
    """
    Smoothed objective:
      val = 2n * LSE_beta(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
    and its gradient w.r.t. a.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        beta = float(beta)
    except Exception:
        beta = 1.0
    beta = max(1e-8, beta)

    shifted = beta * (c - cmax)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(es):
        e = np.zeros_like(c)
        e[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        es = 1.0

    w = e / es
    L = cmax + float(np.log(es)) / beta
    gradL = grad_lse(a, w)

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    grad = 2.0 * n * (gradL / S2 - 2.0 * L / S3)
    val = float(2.0 * n * L / S2)

    if not np.isfinite(val) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return val, grad


def build_p_from_q(q, order):
    """
    Reconstruct p from q variables.

    order[0:-1] are non-baseline coordinates, order[-1] is baseline fixed to 0.
    p = softmax([q, 0]) placed according to order.
    """
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    order = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    n = order.size
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    if n == 1:
        p[order[-1]] = 1.0
        return p

    if q.size and not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        p[order[-1]] = 1.0
        return p

    qmax = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
    zmax = max(0.0, qmax)

    qs = q - zmax
    qs = np.clip(qs, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(qs)
    base = float(np.exp(-zmax)) if zmax < 700.0 else 0.0
    denom = float(e.sum()) + base

    if denom <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(denom):
        p[order[-1]] = 1.0
        return p

    p[order[:-1]] = e / denom
    p[order[-1]] = base / denom
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s > 1e-15 and np.isfinite(s):
        p /= s
    else:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return p


def lse_score_q_and_grad(q, beta, order):
    """Objective and gradient for L-BFGS in softmax q parameterization."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    order = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    n = order.size

    if n <= 1:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    p = build_p_from_q(q, order)
    val, gp = lse_score_and_grad(p, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(val) or gp.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(gp)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    grad_full = p * (gp - dot)
    grad_q = grad_full[order[:-1]]

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_q)):
        grad_q = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(val), grad_q


def log_param_from_p(p):
    """
    Convert p to q variables with a fixed baseline coordinate (the largest p).
    Returns q of length n-1 and order array.
    """
    p = normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)

    b = int(np.argmax(p))
    order = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    order = np.concatenate((order[order != b], np.array([b], dtype=np.int64)))

    base = max(float(p[b]), 1e-300)
    vals = p[order[:-1]]
    q = np.log(np.maximum(vals, 1e-300)) - np.log(base)
    q = np.clip(q, -700.0, 700.0)
    return q, order


def run_lbfgs_q(a0, beta, maxiter=2000, maxfun=6000):
    """
    L-BFGS on softmax log-ratio variables q.
    """
    a0 = normalize(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n <= 1:
        return compute_score(a0), a0

    sc0 = compute_score(a0)
    q0, order = log_param_from_p(a0)
    if q0.size == 0:
        return sc0, a0

    maxiter = int(max(100, min(6000, maxiter)))
    maxfun = int(max(1000, maxfun))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_score_q_and_grad,
            q0,
            args=(float(beta), order),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-18,
                "gtol": 1e-13,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxcor": 10,
                "maxls": 12,
            },
        )
        qopt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        aout = build_p_from_q(qopt, order)
        if aout.size != n or aout.sum() < 1e-12 or not np.all(np.isfinite(aout)):
            return sc0, a0
        scout = compute_score(aout)
        if not np.isfinite(scout):
            return sc0, a0
        return scout, aout
    except Exception:
        return sc0, a0


def run_lbfgs_p(a0, beta, maxiter=1500, maxfun=5000):
    """
    Fallback L-BFGS directly on p with nonnegative bounds.
    """
    a0 = normalize(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n <= 1:
        return compute_score(a0), a0

    sc0 = compute_score(a0)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n

    maxiter = int(max(100, min(6000, maxiter)))
    maxfun = int(max(1000, maxfun))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_score_and_grad,
            a0,
            args=(float(beta),),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-18,
                "gtol": 1e-13,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxcor": 10,
                "maxls": 12,
            },
        )
        aout = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0, 1000.0)
        if aout.size != n or aout.sum() < 1e-12 or not np.all(np.isfinite(aout)):
            return sc0, a0
        scout = compute_score(aout)
        if not np.isfinite(scout):
            return sc0, a0
        return scout, aout
    except Exception:
        return sc0, a0


def fienup_flat(a, n_iters=50):
    """Fienup: gently flatten the power spectrum to diversify the basin."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    M = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)

    for it in range(n_iters):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
        P = A.real * A.real + A.imag * A.imag
        Pmean = float(P.mean())
        alpha = 0.12 + 0.68 * (float(it) / float(max(1, n_iters)))
        Pnew = (1.0 - alpha) * P + alpha * Pmean
        Pnew = np.maximum(Pnew, 1e-30)
        ratio = np.sqrt(Pnew / np.maximum(P, 1e-30))
        Anew = A * ratio
        anew = np.fft.irfft(Anew, M)[:n]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(anew)):
            break
        anew = normalize(anew)
        sc = compute_score(anew)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = anew.copy()
    return best_a


def fienup_quantile(a, n_iters=50):
    """Fienup with spectral clipping."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    M = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)

    for it in range(n_iters):
        prog = float(it) / float(max(1, n_iters))
        q = 0.65 + 0.25 * prog
        thresh_factor = 1.05 - 0.35 * prog
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
        P = A.real * A.real + A.imag * A.imag
        thresh = thresh_factor * float(np.quantile(P, q))
        Pnew = P.copy()
        above = Pnew > thresh
        Pnew[above] = thresh
        Pnew = np.maximum(Pnew, 1e-30)
        ratio = np.sqrt(Pnew / np.maximum(P, 1e-30))
        Anew = A * ratio
        anew = np.fft.irfft(Anew, M)[:n]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(anew)):
            break
        anew = normalize(anew)
        sc = compute_score(anew)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = anew.copy()
    return best_a


def perturb_add(a, rng, scale=0.01):
    """Small additive Gaussian perturbation."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    new = a + float(scale) * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return normalize(new)


def perturb_mult(a, rng, scale=0.03):
    """Low-frequency multiplicative perturbation."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    pert = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 5):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 15))
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        amp = float(scale) / float(0.7 + m)
        pert += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        pert += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph + 0.7)
    new = a * (1.0 + pert)
    return normalize(new)


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    """Resample sequence to new length and normalize."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if n == 0 or n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    anew = np.interp(xnew, xold, a)
    return normalize(anew)


def make_initial_no_parent(n, rng):
    """Fallback construction if no parent exists."""
    n = int(max(64, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    candidates = []
    candidates.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    for pw in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        candidates.append(np.maximum((x ** pw), 1e-3))
        candidates.append(np.maximum(((1.0 - x) ** pw), 1e-3))
    for lam in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        candidates.append(np.exp(-lam * x) + 1e-3)
        candidates.append(np.exp(-lam * (1.0 - x)) + 1e-3)
    for c, w in ((0.25, 0.1), (0.75, 0.1)):
        candidates.append(np.exp(-((x - c) / w) ** 2) + 0.15)
    for c1, w1, c2, w2 in ((0.2, 0.08, 0.7, 0.12), (0.3, 0.12, 0.8, 0.08)):
        candidates.append(
            np.exp(-((x - c1) / w1) ** 2) + 1.3 * np.exp(-((x - c2) / w2) ** 2) + 0.1
        )

    best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    best_sc = compute_score(best_a)
    for cand in candidates:
        p = normalize(np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64))
        sc = compute_score(p)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = p.copy()
    return best_a


def make_exchange_candidate(
    a,
    g,
    pos_order_ga,
    pos_order_g,
    neg_order,
    kpos,
    kadd,
    frac,
    pos_mode,
    add_mode
):
    """
    Build a concrete mass-transfer candidate.

    Remove frac of the mass from selected positive-gradient coordinates and
    add the same total mass to selected negative-gradient coordinates.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None

    kpos = int(min(max(1, kpos), n // 2))
    kadd = int(min(max(1, kadd), n))
    frac = float(min(0.5, max(1e-4, frac)))

    if pos_mode == 0:
        pos_all = pos_order_ga
    else:
        pos_all = pos_order_g

    pos = pos_all[:kpos]
    pos = pos[(g[pos] > 1e-12) & (a[pos] > 1e-12)]
    if pos.size == 0:
        return None

    if a[pos].sum() < 1e-5 and pos.size < pos_all.size:
        extra = min(pos_all.size, max(2 * kpos, kpos + min(kpos, 32)))
        pos = pos_all[:extra]
        pos = pos[(g[pos] > 1e-12) & (a[pos] > 1e-12)]
        if pos.size == 0:
            return None

    rem = frac * a[pos]
    m = float(rem.sum())
    if m < 1e-12:
        return None

    pnew = a.copy()
    pnew[pos] = a[pos] - rem

    neg = neg_order[:kadd]
    neg = neg[g[neg] < -1e-12]
    if neg.size == 0:
        return None

    if add_mode == 0:
        weights = np.ones(neg.size, dtype=np.float64)
    elif add_mode == 1:
        weights = -g[neg]
    else:
        weights = (-g[neg]) * (a[neg] + 1e-8)

    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64)
    weights[weights < 0.0] = 0.0
    wsum = float(weights.sum())
    if wsum <= 1e-30:
        weights = np.ones(neg.size, dtype=np.float64)
        wsum = float(neg.size)

    add = m * weights / wsum

    mean_a = 1.0 / float(n)
    max_add = min(0.005, max(0.0002, 60.0 * mean_a))
    if add.size and add.max() > max_add:
        add = np.minimum(add, max_add)
        for _ in range(4):
            deficit = m - float(add.sum())
            if deficit <= 1e-14:
                break
            mask = add < max_add
            if not mask.any():
                break
            wmask = weights[mask]
            wmsum = float(wmask.sum())
            if wmsum > 1e-30:
                add[mask] += deficit * wmask / wmsum
            else:
                add[mask] += deficit / float(mask.sum())

    pnew[neg] = pnew[neg] + add
    pnew = np.maximum(pnew, 0.0)
    s = float(pnew.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    pnew = pnew / s
    return pnew


def exchange_mass(a, beta, max_iters, deadline, rng, num_cands=2):
    """
    Greedy mass-exchange local search driven by LSE gradients.
    Returns (best_sequence, best_score, current_sequence, current_score).
    """
    a = normalize(a.copy())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf, a, np.inf

    cur_score = compute_score(a)
    best_score = cur_score
    best_a = a.copy()
    it = 0
    no_imp = 0
    params = EXCHANGE_PARAMS

    while it < max_iters and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = fast_conv(a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * n * cmax

        if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
            cur_score = sc
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        shift = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
        shift = np.clip(shift, -700.0, 700.0)
        e = np.exp(shift)
        es = float(e.sum())
        if es <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(es):
            break

        w = e / es
        g = 2.0 * n * grad_lse(a, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        # Tangential gradient on the simplex.
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if (float(np.max(g)) - float(np.min(g))) <= 1e-13 * max(1.0, gscale):
            no_imp += 6
        else:
            no_imp = 0 if (no_imp == 0) else max(0, no_imp - 3)

        gpos = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        pos_order_g = np.argsort(-gpos)
        pos_score = gpos * a
        pos_order_ga = np.argsort(-pos_score)
        neg_order = np.argsort(-np.minimum(g, 0.0))

        improved = False
        cidx = int(rng.integers(len(params)))
        for _ in range(max(1, int(num_cands))):
            param = params[cidx % len(params)]
            cidx = int(rng.integers(len(params)))
            pnew = make_exchange_candidate(
                a,
                g,
                pos_order_ga,
                pos_order_g,
                neg_order,
                param[0],
                param[1],
                param[2],
                param[3],
                param[4],
            )
            if pnew is None:
                continue
            sc2 = compute_score(pnew)
            if sc2 < cur_score - 1e-12:
                a = pnew
                cur_score = sc2
                improved = True
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_a = pnew.copy()

        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        no_imp += 1
        if no_imp >= 22:
            no_imp = 0
            scale = 0.001 + 0.004 * float(int(rng.random() * 4))
            a2 = perturb_mult(best_a, rng, scale)
            sc2 = compute_score(a2)
            if sc2 < best_score * 1.02 and sc2 < cur_score * 1.05:
                a = a2
                cur_score = sc2
            else:
                a2 = perturb_add(best_a, rng, 0.0005)
                sc2 = compute_score(a2)
                if sc2 < cur_score:
                    a = a2
                    cur_score = sc2
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score
        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score, a, cur_score


def quick_exchange_polish(a, budget_s, deadline, rng):
    """Short exchange-only polish, useful for larger-n trials."""
    a = normalize(a.copy())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    cur_a = a.copy()
    stop = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + float(max(5.0, budget_s)))

    betas = (30.0, 200.0, 900.0, 4000.0)
    for beta in betas:
        rem = stop - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        maxit = int(min(100000, max(300, rem * 25)))
        try:
            b_a, b_sc, cur_a, cur_sc = exchange_mass(
                cur_a, beta, maxit, stop, rng, num_cands=1
            )
            if b_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = b_sc
                best_a = b_a.copy()
                cur_a = best_a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_a, best_sc


def optimize_start(a, budget_s, deadline, rng, verbose=False, exchange_heavy=False):
    """
    One local optimization campaign from a starting sequence.
    """
    a = normalize(a.copy())
    init_score = compute_score(a)
    best_score = init_score
    best_a = a.copy()
    cur_a = a.copy()

    stop = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + float(max(5.0, budget_s)))
    n = a.size
    rate_lbfgs = 4.0 if n <= 8000 else 2.5

    if verbose:
        print(f"  optimize_start: n={n}, init={init_score:.8f}, budget={budget_s:.0f}s")

    if exchange_heavy:
        lbfgs_betas = (8.0, 60.0, 400.0)
        exchange_betas = (30.0, 100.0, 400.0, 1500.0, 5000.0, 15000.0)
    else:
        lbfgs_betas = (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 400.0, 2000.0)
        exchange_betas = (20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0)

    # Phase 1: L-BFGS coarse-to-fine.
    for idx, beta in enumerate(lbfgs_betas):
        rem = stop - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 6.0:
            break
        remaining_betas = len(lbfgs_betas) - idx
        seg = min(35.0, rem / float(max(1, remaining_betas)))
        maxiter = int(min(3000, max(250, seg * rate_lbfgs)))
        maxfun = int(max(3000, maxiter * 3))
        try:
            sc, p = run_lbfgs_q(cur_a, beta, maxiter, maxfun)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = p.copy()
                cur_a = p.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Fallback if L-BFGS was ineffective and time remains.
    rem = stop - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 45.0 and best_score > init_score - 1e-8:
        try:
            sc, p = run_lbfgs_p(
                best_a,
                300.0,
                int(min(1500, max(300, rem * 2.0))),
                int(min(5000, max(3000, rem * 6.0)))
            )
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = p.copy()
                cur_a = p.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase 2: exchange mass local search.
    for idx, beta in enumerate(exchange_betas):
        rem = stop - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        remaining_betas = len(exchange_betas) - idx
        seg = min(140.0, rem / float(max(1, remaining_betas)))
        if beta < 500.0:
            maxit = int(min(50000, max(400, seg * 70.0)))
            num_cands = 2 if beta < 200.0 else 1
        elif beta < 5000.0:
            maxit = int(min(50000, max(400, seg * 45.0)))
            num_cands = 1
        else:
            maxit = int(min(50000, max(400, seg * 30.0)))
            num_cands = 1
        try:
            b_a, b_sc, cur_a, cur_sc = exchange_mass(
                cur_a, beta, maxit, stop, rng, num_cands=num_cands
            )
            if b_sc < best_score:
                best_score = b_sc
                best_a = b_a.copy()
                cur_a = best_a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
        cur_a = best_a.copy()

    # Phase 3: final short polish.
    rem = stop - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 10.0:
        try:
            b_a, b_sc, cur_a, cur_sc = exchange_mass(
                best_a,
                5000.0,
                int(min(100000, max(200, rem * 40.0))),
                stop,
                rng,
                num_cands=1
            )
            if b_sc < best_score:
                best_score = b_sc
                best_a = b_a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    rem = stop - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        try:
            sc, p = run_lbfgs_q(
                best_a,
                8000.0,
                int(min(3000, max(400, rem * 2.5))),
                int(min(10000, max(3000, rem * 5.0)))
            )
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = p.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if verbose:
        print(f"  optimize_start done: best={best_score:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Generate the best nonnegative height sequence found within budget_s seconds.
    """
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s < 120.0:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load parent if available.
    a0 = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size >= 16 and float(np.sum(arr)) > 1e-12 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                a0 = normalize(arr)
    except Exception:
        a0 = None

    if a0 is None:
        a0 = make_initial_no_parent(8192, rng)

    best_score = compute_score(a0)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    print(f"Starting score: {best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_a)}")

    # Generate a small diverse start pool.
    start_list = [(best_score, a0.copy())]
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 250.0:
        try:
            fa = fienup_flat(a0, n_iters=45)
            start_list.append((compute_score(fa), fa))
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            fq = fienup_quantile(a0, n_iters=45)
            start_list.append((compute_score(fq), fq))
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            p1 = perturb_mult(a0, rng, 0.035)
            start_list.append((compute_score(p1), p1))
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            p2 = perturb_mult(a0, rng, 0.09)
            start_list.append((compute_score(p2), p2))
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            p3 = perturb_add(a0, rng, 0.012)
            start_list.append((compute_score(p3), p3))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Sort starts by initial score; stable order keeps parent present among ties.
    start_list.sort(key=start_sort_key)
    final_starts = start_list[:4]

    safe = max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    reserve = min(320.0, max(90.0, safe * 0.13))
    start_budget = max(60.0, safe - reserve)
    n_use = min(4, len(final_starts))
    weights = [0.42, 0.26, 0.18, 0.14]
    weights = weights[:n_use]
    wsum = float(sum(weights)) if weights else 1.0

    for idx in range(n_use):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 15.0:
            break
        st_score, st_a = final_starts[idx]
        alloc = start_budget * (float(weights[idx]) / wsum)
        if idx > 0 and st_score > best_score + 0.12:
            alloc *= 0.45
        alloc = min(alloc, max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
        if alloc < 20.0:
            continue

        try:
            b_a, b_sc = optimize_start(
                st_a,
                alloc,
                deadline,
                rng,
                verbose=True,
                exchange_heavy=(idx == 0),
            )
            if b_sc < best_score:
                best_score = b_sc
                best_a = b_a.copy()
                print(f"  New global best after start {idx}: {best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Larger-n exploration: length up to 16000 still uses the same 32768 FFT.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 320.0:
        n_cur = len(best_a)
        target_n = None
        if n_cur < 12500:
            target_n = 12500
        elif n_cur < 16000 and rem > 420.0:
            target_n = 16000
        elif n_cur < 16000:
            target_n = 14000

        if target_n is not None and target_n > n_cur + 256:
            try:
                a_large = interpolate_sequence(best_a, target_n)
                sc_large = compute_score(a_large)
                if sc_large < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_large
                    best_a = a_large.copy()
                    print(f"  Larger n={target_n} interpolated: {best_score:.8f}")
                elif sc_large < best_score + 0.004:
                    # Often worth polishing even if not immediately better.
                    best_score_tmp = max(best_score, sc_large)
                    trial_a = best_a if sc_large > best_score else a_large
                    big_alloc = min(160.0, max(20.0, rem - 220.0))
                    b_a, b_sc = quick_exchange_polish(trial_a, big_alloc, deadline, rng)
                    if b_sc < best_score:
                        best_score = b_sc
                        best_a = b_a.copy()
                        print(f"  Larger n={target_n} polished: {best_score:.8f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final reserve polish on the global best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 35.0:
        try:
            b_a, b_sc = optimize_start(
                best_a,
                max(10.0, rem - 20.0),
                deadline,
                rng,
                verbose=True,
                exchange_heavy=True,
            )
            if b_sc < best_score:
                best_score = b_sc
                best_a = b_a.copy()
                print(f"  Final polish improved: {best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Build output. Scale to sum sqrt(2n) for numerical stability; score is invariant.
    n = len(best_a)
    if n == 0 or best_score == np.inf:
        out = [1.0] * 64
    else:
        scale = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
        out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64) * scale
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
        if sum(out) < 0.01:
            out = [1.0] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_score

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"Internal best: {best_score:.10f}")
    print(f"Verified score: {verified:.10f}")
    print(f"Length: {len(out)}")
    print(f"Time used: {time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s of {budget_s:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return out
```
